 GA 01 

 Majuscule

 4th Century

 View 

 Codex Sinaiticus is a fourth century manuscript of the Greek Old Testament, the New Testament, the Epistle of Barnabas, and the Shepherd of Hermas on parchment; The facsimile images are from the J. T. and Zelma Luther Archives, A. Webb Roberts Library, Southwestern Baptist Theological Seminary. For more recent digital images, please visit the Codex Sinaiticus Project 

 Location: London, British Library

 Shelf Number: Add. 43725

 GA 02 

 Majuscule

 5th Century

 View 

 Codex Alexandrinus is a fifth century manuscript of the Greek Old Testament, the New Testament, and the Clementine Epistles on parchment. Images are from the 1879–1883 and 1909 full-sized black and white facsimiles produced by the British Museum. For more recent images, please visit the British Museum 

 Location: London, British Library

 Shelf Number: Royal 1 D.VIII

 GA 03 

 Majuscule

 4th Century

 View 

 Codex Vaticanus is an important fourth century majuscule manuscript. It contains Matthew–2 Thessalonians, Hebrews 1.1–9.13, James–Jude. It lacks the Pastorals, Philemon, and Revelation. After Hebrews 9.13, the document is written in much later minuscule hand. 142 leaves on parchment, three columns, with 42 lines per column. The images are from the 1868 (pseudo-) facsimile. For more recent images visit the Vatican's website .

 Location: Vatican Library

 Shelf Number: Vat. gr. 1209, p. 1235-1518 (p. 1519-1536: 1957)

 GA 04 

 Majuscule

 5th Century

 View 

 Codex Ephraemi Rescriptus is a fifth century palimpsest manuscript of the Gospels, Apostolos, Paul, and Revelation on parchment; 145 leaves, single column, 41-44 lines per column. For recent images, please visit the Bibliothèque Nationale .

 Location: Bibliothèque Nationale, Paris

 Shelf Number: Gr. 9

 GA 0240 

 Majuscule

 5th Century

 View 

 Fifth century majuscule palimpsest of Paul on parchment. The under-text is in Greek (GA 0240) and the over-text is in Georgian. The codex has parchment and papyrus leaves interspersed throughout and is bound in three separate volumes. GA 0240 is found in volumes 2 & 3. 

 Location: Tbilisi, National Centre of Manuscripts

 Shelf Number: NCM 2123

 GA Lect 358 

 Lectionary

 10th Century

 View 

 Tenth century lectionary of the Gospels on parchment; 2 leaves, 2 columns, 21 lines per column. Digital images are from microfilm. Lect 358 (formerly Lect 1904) is found in folios 59-60 of this codex. This codex also contains the following: 
Lect 1896, Lect 1897, Lect 1898, Lect 1899, Lect 1900, Lect 1901, Lect 1902, Lect 1903, Lect 1905, Lect 1906, Lect 1907. 

Part of this manuscript is also held at the Bibliothèque Nationale in Paris.

 Location: Athos, Vatopediu

 Shelf Number: 1219, fol. 59.60
